Yes, sir, I have. There was... well, a quiet month or so, not too long ago. I was sleeping just fine, normal hours, and I didn't have any of those big plans or restless energy. I don't sleep much at all during those times, and I get to making all sorts of big plans. \n\nBut the thing is, even when I'm feeling all wound up and high like that, I still hear my friend's voice. And I still feel like those men at the domino table or the radio announcer are sending me those warnings. It doesn't seem to stop, no matter how my mood is doing.\n\nDoctor: Have there ever been times when the messages or voices kept happening for at least two weeks when your mood was completely back to normal?\n\nPatient: Yes, there was. I remember... well, there was a whole month where things were quiet with my mood. I was sleeping just fine, getting a regular night's rest, and I wasn't getting all wound up with those big plans I mentioned. I felt pretty normal, I guess you'd say. \n\nBut even then... I still heard my friend talking to me out on the porch. And I still felt certain that the man on the radio was sending those messages meant just for me. So, yes, the voices and the messages kept right on going, even when my mood was settled down for that whole month.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed a decline in your work, social life, or ability to take care of yourself because of these symptoms?\n\nPatient: Well, yes sir, I suppose I have. I don't work anymore, of course, being my age, but... my regular day-to-day just ain't what it was. \n\nI've been missing my choir rehearsals lately. And out back, well, I've just let parts of my garden go to weeds. I just haven't kept up with it like I should. And those domino games with the fellas I was telling you about... I guess I made things uncomfortable enough down there that I just don't join in like I used to. It's just been easier to stay away.\n\nDoctor: When you are not having one of those high or depressed mood periods, do you still hear voices talking to you?\n\nPatient: Yes, I do. Even when my mood is just fine, like I mentioned, I still hear things. Some afternoons, when the house is real quiet, I'll hear my old friend out on the porch. He'll call my name, or sometimes he'll just be humming one of those choir songs we used to sing. It sounds so clear, just like he's standing right there next to me.\n\nDoctor: Have there been times when your mood was clearly high or depressed for a long stretch of time while the voices or messages were still happening?\n\nPatient: Yes, they sure do. Even when I have those... well, those bright, restless weeks I told you about. The times when I'm hardly sleeping at all and I'm busy making all sorts of big plans. \n\nEven right in the middle of all that, I still hear my friend's voice clear as day. And I still get the strong feeling that the radio, or those men down at the domino game, are sending me warnings. So, yes sir, the voices and the messages keep right on going even when my mood is all wound up like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever gone for at least two weeks hearing voices or believing these messages were meant for you when you were not having any mood episode at all—no manic symptoms and no depression?\n\nPatient: Yes, sir, I have. There was... well, a quiet month or so, not too long ago. I was sleeping just fine, normal hours, and I didn't have any of those big plans or restless energy. My mood was just steady, plain as can be. But even then, I'd still hear my friend out on the porch, clear as a bell. And I still knew the radio was sending those messages to me. So, yes, it happens even when my mood is perfectly regular.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed yourself becoming less emotionally expressive or more emotionally flat over time?\n\nPatient: Well, yes sir, I reckon I have. I mean, I still love my family just as much as ever, don't get me wrong. But... well, a lot of days now, I just sit there at the kitchen table. I don't really have much to say to anybody. I just sort of sit there and let things go by. Like my garden, or the choir practice... I just let them go by without feeling like moving to do any of it. I just don't have the drive or the feeling to get up and do much.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least two weeks when you felt clearly depressed most of the day nearly every day?\n\nPatient: No, sir, I can't say that I have. I mean, I have my quiet days like I mentioned, where I just sit and don't do much. But I wouldn't call it feeling sad or down in the dumps. I just feel... still, I suppose. But not depressed like you're asking about.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least one week when you felt so unusually energized or irritable that you were much more active or talkative than usual?\n\nPatient: Yes, sir, I have. There are some weeks where I wake up feeling just... real bright and important, I guess you could say. I'll be laughing loud and talking mighty fast to folks. But I'll tell you, if somebody tries to slow me down or starts questioning what I'm doing when I'm like that, I get pretty sharp with them. So, yes, I do get those periods where I'm a lot more energized and talkative.\n\nDoctor: Have there been times when you heard voices or got those messages when you were not having any mood symptoms at all, neither feeling unusually energized nor depressed?\n\nPatient: Yes, sir, there was. I remember a spell... maybe a whole month where things were just real quiet. I was sleeping my normal hours, and I wasn't full of those big plans or feeling all wound up. Just regular. \n\nBut even then, I still heard my friend talking out on the porch. And I still believed the radio was speaking right to me. So, yes, I get those things even when my mood ain't acting up.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. So, yes, the voices and the messages kept right on going, even when my mood was settled down for that whole month.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed a decline in your work, social life, or ability to take care of yourself because of these symptoms?\n\nPatient: Well, yes sir, I suppose I have. I don't work anymore, of course, being my age, but... my regular day-to-day just ain't what it was. \n\nI've been missing my choir rehearsals lately. And out back, well, I've just let parts of my garden go to weeds. I just haven't kept up with it like I should. And those domino games with the fellas I was telling you about... I guess I made things uncomfortable enough down there that I just don't join in like I used to. It's just been easier to stay away.\n\nDoctor: When you are not having one of those high or depressed mood periods, do you still hear voices talking to you?\n\nPatient: Yes, I do. Even when my mood is just fine, like I mentioned, I still hear things. Some afternoons, when the house is real quiet, I'll hear my old friend out on the porch. He'll call my name, or sometimes he'll just be humming one of those choir songs we used to sing. It sounds so clear, just like he's standing right there next to me.\n\nDoctor: Have there been times when your mood was clearly high or depressed for a long stretch of time while the voices or messages were still happening?\n\nPatient: Yes, they sure do. Even when I have those... well, those bright, restless weeks I told you about. The times when I'm hardly sleeping at all and I'm busy making all sorts of big plans. \n\nEven right in the middle of all that, I still hear my friend's voice clear as day. And I still get the strong feeling that the radio, or those men down at the domino game, are sending me warnings. So, yes sir, the voices and the messages keep right on going even when my mood is all wound up like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever gone for at least two weeks hearing voices or believing these messages were meant for you when you were not having any mood episode at all—no manic symptoms and no depression?\n\nPatient: Yes, sir, I have. There was... well, a quiet month or so, not too long ago. I was sleeping just fine, normal hours, and I didn't have any of those big plans or restless energy. My mood was just steady, plain as can be. But even then, I'd still hear my friend out on the porch, clear as a bell. And I still knew the radio was sending those messages to me. So, yes, it happens even when my mood is perfectly regular.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed yourself becoming less emotionally expressive or more emotionally flat over time?\n\nPatient: Well, yes sir, I reckon I have. I mean, I still love my family just as much as ever, don't get me wrong. But... well, a lot of days now, I just sit there at the kitchen table. I don't really have much to say to anybody. I just sort of sit there and let things go by. Like my garden, or the choir practice... I just let them go by without feeling like moving to do any of it. I just don't have the drive or the feeling to get up and do much.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least two weeks when you felt clearly depressed most of the day nearly every day?\n\nPatient: No, sir, I can't say that I have. I mean, I have my quiet days like I mentioned, where I just sit and don't do much. But I wouldn't call it feeling sad or down in the dumps. I just feel... still, I suppose. But not depressed like you're asking about.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least one week when you felt so unusually energized or irritable that you were much more active or talkative than usual?\n\nPatient: Yes, sir, I have. There are some weeks where I wake up feeling just... real bright and important, I guess you could say. I'll be laughing loud and talking mighty fast to folks. But I'll tell you, if somebody tries to slow me down or starts questioning what I'm doing when I'm like that, I get pretty sharp with them. So, yes, I do get those periods where I'm a lot more energized and talkative.\n\nDoctor: Have there been times when you heard voices or got those messages when you were not having any mood symptoms at all, neither feeling unusually energized nor depressed?\n\nPatient: Yes, sir, there was. I remember a spell... maybe a whole month where things were just real quiet. I was sleeping my normal hours, and I wasn't full of those big plans or feeling all wound up. Just regular. \n\nBut even then, I still heard my friend talking out on the porch. And I still believed the radio was speaking right to me. So, yes, I get those things even when my mood ain't acting up.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least two weeks when the voices or messages were gone completely?\n\nPatient: No, sir, I don't believe I have. They don't ever really leave me be for that long. I mean, maybe a day or two might go by where it's a bit quieter, but... well, no, not two whole weeks. That friend on the porch and the things on the radio, they're pretty much always around in some way.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. It doesn't seem to stop, no matter how my mood is doing.\n\nDoctor: Have there ever been times when the messages or voices kept happening for at least two weeks when your mood was completely back to normal?\n\nPatient: Yes, there was. I remember... well, there was a whole month where things were quiet with my mood. I was sleeping just fine, getting a regular night's rest, and I wasn't getting all wound up with those big plans I mentioned. I felt pretty normal, I guess you'd say. \n\nBut even then... I still heard my friend talking to me out on the porch. And I still felt certain that the man on the radio was sending those messages meant just for me. So, yes, the voices and the messages kept right on going, even when my mood was settled down for that whole month.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed a decline in your work, social life, or ability to take care of yourself because of these symptoms?\n\nPatient: Well, yes sir, I suppose I have. I don't work anymore, of course, being my age, but... my regular day-to-day just ain't what it was. \n\nI've been missing my choir rehearsals lately. And out back, well, I've just let parts of my garden go to weeds. I just haven't kept up with it like I should. And those domino games with the fellas I was telling you about... I guess I made things uncomfortable enough down there that I just don't join in like I used to. It's just been easier to stay away.\n\nDoctor: When you are not having one of those high or depressed mood periods, do you still hear voices talking to you?\n\nPatient: Yes, I do. Even when my mood is just fine, like I mentioned, I still hear things. Some afternoons, when the house is real quiet, I'll hear my old friend out on the porch. He'll call my name, or sometimes he'll just be humming one of those choir songs we used to sing. It sounds so clear, just like he's standing right there next to me.\n\nDoctor: Have there been times when your mood was clearly high or depressed for a long stretch of time while the voices or messages were still happening?\n\nPatient: Yes, they sure do. Even when I have those... well, those bright, restless weeks I told you about. The times when I'm hardly sleeping at all and I'm busy making all sorts of big plans. \n\nEven right in the middle of all that, I still hear my friend's voice clear as day. And I still get the strong feeling that the radio, or those men down at the domino game, are sending me warnings. So, yes sir, the voices and the messages keep right on going even when my mood is all wound up like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever gone for at least two weeks hearing voices or believing these messages were meant for you when you were not having any mood episode at all—no manic symptoms and no depression?\n\nPatient: Yes, sir, I have. There was... well, a quiet month or so, not too long ago. I was sleeping just fine, normal hours, and I didn't have any of those big plans or restless energy. My mood was just steady, plain as can be. But even then, I'd still hear my friend out on the porch, clear as a bell. And I still knew the radio was sending those messages to me. So, yes, it happens even when my mood is perfectly regular.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed yourself becoming less emotionally expressive or more emotionally flat over time?\n\nPatient: Well, yes sir, I reckon I have. I mean, I still love my family just as much as ever, don't get me wrong. But... well, a lot of days now, I just sit there at the kitchen table. I don't really have much to say to anybody. I just sort of sit there and let things go by. Like my garden, or the choir practice... I just let them go by without feeling like moving to do any of it. I just don't have the drive or the feeling to get up and do much.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least two weeks when you felt clearly depressed most of the day nearly every day?\n\nPatient: No, sir, I can't say that I have. So, yes, I do get those periods where I'm a lot more energized and talkative.\n\nDoctor: Have there been times when you heard voices or got those messages when you were not having any mood symptoms at all, neither feeling unusually energized nor depressed?\n\nPatient: Yes, sir, there was. Just regular. \n\nBut even then, I still heard my friend talking out on the porch. And I still believed the radio was speaking right to me. So, yes, I get those things even when my mood ain't acting up.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least two weeks when the voices or messages were gone completely?\n\nPatient: No, sir, I don't believe I have. They don't ever really leave me be for that long. I mean, maybe a day or two might go by where it's a bit quieter, but... well, no, not two whole weeks. That friend on the porch and the things on the radio, they're pretty much always around in some way.\n\nDoctor: Have there been any times when the voices or messages were only present during your mood episodes and completely absent when your mood was normal?\n\nPatient: No, sir, it's not like that. I just sort of sit there and let things go by. Like my garden, or the choir practice... I just let them go by without feeling like moving to do any of it. I just don't have the drive or the feeling to get up and do much.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least two weeks when you felt clearly depressed most of the day nearly every day?\n\nPatient: No, sir, I can't say that I have. But not depressed like you're asking about.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least one week when you felt so unusually energized or irritable that you were much more active or talkative than usual?\n\nPatient: Yes, sir, I have. So, yes, I do get those periods where I'm a lot more energized and talkative.\n\nDoctor: Have there been times when you heard voices or got those messages when you were not having any mood symptoms at all, neither feeling unusually energized nor depressed?\n\nPatient: Yes, sir, there was. What brings you in?\n\nPatient: Well, Doctor, I came in because of some things going on. It's... well, it's the men down at the domino table, mostly. And sometimes the announcer on the radio, too. That's why I'm here.\n\nDoctor: Have you used any alcohol or drugs, or started any new medications, before these messages began?\n\nPatient: No, sir. I don't drink, and I haven't taken any kind of drugs. As for medications, I'm just taking the same pills for my blood pressure that I've been taking for years. Nothing new at all.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any periods when your mood was unusually high or depressed around the time these messages started?\n\nPatient: Well, yes, I suppose I do. Sometimes I get these... I guess you could call them bright weeks. Just real restless. I don't sleep much at all during those times, and I get to making all sorts of big plans. \n\nBut the thing is, even when I'm feeling all wound up and high like that, I still hear my friend's voice. And I still feel like those men at the domino table or the radio announcer are sending me those warnings. It doesn't seem to stop, no matter how my mood is doing.\n\nDoctor: Have there ever been times when the messages or voices kept happening for at least two weeks when your mood was completely back to normal?\n\nPatient: Yes, there was. I remember... well, there was a whole month where things were quiet with my mood. I was sleeping just fine, getting a regular night's rest, and I wasn't getting all wound up with those big plans I mentioned. I felt pretty normal, I guess you'd say. \n\nBut even then... I still heard my friend talking to me out on the porch. And I still felt certain that the man on the radio was sending those messages meant just for me. So, yes, the voices and the messages kept right on going, even when my mood was settled down for that whole month.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed a decline in your work, social life, or ability to take care of yourself because of these symptoms?\n\nPatient: Well, yes sir, I suppose I have. I don't work anymore, of course, being my age, but... my regular day-to-day just ain't what it was. \n\nI've been missing my choir rehearsals lately. And out back, well, I've just let parts of my garden go to weeds. I just haven't kept up with it like I should. And those domino games with the fellas I was telling you about... I guess I made things uncomfortable enough down there that I just don't join in like I used to. It's just been easier to stay away.\n\nDoctor: When you are not having one of those high or depressed mood periods, do you still hear voices talking to you?\n\nPatient: Yes, I do. Even when my mood is just fine, like I mentioned, I still hear things. Some afternoons, when the house is real quiet, I'll hear my old friend out on the porch. He'll call my name, or sometimes he'll just be humming one of those choir songs we used to sing. It sounds so clear, just like he's standing right there next to me.\n\nDoctor: Have there been times when your mood was clearly high or depressed for a long stretch of time while the voices or messages were still happening?\n\nPatient: Yes, they sure do. Even when I have those... well, those bright, restless weeks I told you about. The times when I'm hardly sleeping at all and I'm busy making all sorts of big plans. \n\nEven right in the middle of all that, I still hear my friend's voice clear as day. And I still get the strong feeling that the radio, or those men down at the domino game, are sending me warnings. So, yes sir, the voices and the messages keep right on going even when my mood is all wound up like that.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever gone for at least two weeks hearing voices or believing these messages were meant for you when you were not having any mood episode at all—no manic symptoms and no depression?\n\nPatient: Yes, sir, I have. There was... well, a quiet month or so, not too long ago. I was sleeping just fine, normal hours, and I didn't have any of those big plans or restless energy. My mood was just steady, plain as can be. But even then, I'd still hear my friend out on the porch, clear as a bell. And I still knew the radio was sending those messages to me. So, yes, it happens even when my mood is perfectly regular.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed yourself becoming less emotionally expressive or more emotionally flat over time?\n\nPatient: Well, yes sir, I reckon I have. I mean, I still love my family just as much as ever, don't get me wrong. But... well, a lot of days now, I just sit there at the kitchen table. I don't really have much to say to anybody. I just sort of sit there and let things go by. Like my garden, or the choir practice... I just let them go by without feeling like moving to do any of it. I just don't have the drive or the feeling to get up and do much.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least two weeks when you felt clearly depressed most of the day nearly every day?\n\nPatient: No, sir, I can't say that I have. I mean, I have my quiet days like I mentioned, where I just sit and don't do much. But I wouldn't call it feeling sad or down in the dumps. I just feel... still, I suppose. But not depressed like you're asking about.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least one week when you felt so unusually energized or irritable that you were much more active or talkative than usual?\n\nPatient: Yes, sir, I have. There are some weeks where I wake up feeling just... real bright and important, I guess you could say. I'll be laughing loud and talking mighty fast to folks. But I'll tell you, if somebody tries to slow me down or starts questioning what I'm doing when I'm like that, I get pretty sharp with them. So, yes, I do get those periods where I'm a lot more energized and talkative.\n\nDoctor: Have there been times when you heard voices or got those messages when you were not having any mood symptoms at all, neither feeling unusually energized nor depressed?\n\nPatient: Yes, sir, there was. I remember a spell... maybe a whole month where things were just real quiet. I was sleeping my normal hours, and I wasn't full of those big plans or feeling all wound up. Just regular. \n\nBut even then, I still heard my friend talking out on the porch. And I still believed the radio was speaking right to me. So, yes, I get those things even when my mood ain't acting up.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least two weeks when the voices or messages were gone completely?\n\nPatient: No, sir, I don't believe I have. They don't ever really leave me be for that long. I mean, maybe a day or two might go by where it's a bit quieter, but... well, no, not two whole weeks. That friend on the porch and the things on the radio, they're pretty much always around in some way.\n\nDoctor: Have there been any times when the voices or messages were only present during your mood episodes and completely absent when your mood was normal?\n\nPatient: No, sir, it's not like that. Like I was saying just a minute ago... there was that whole month where my mood was just fine. I was sleeping regular, not wound up or making big plans. But even during that quiet month, the voices didn't stop. I was still hearing my friend out on the porch, and I still knew the radio was talking to me. So they definitely don't just happen when my mood is acting up.\n\nDoctor: Over the entire time you’ve had these symptoms, have the high or low mood episodes taken up most of the time, more than the voices and messages have?\n\nPatient: Well, sir... let me think on that a second. I suppose, if I look back since all this started... yes, they have. It seems like more months have been taken up by those high-energy spells, and the aftereffects of 'em, than by my calmer stretches. The voices are always sort of there in the background, like I said, but those big mood spells definitely eat up the biggest part of my time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of at least two weeks when you were hearing voices or believing messages were meant for you, while you were not having any manic or depressive symptoms at all?\n\nPatient: Yes, sir, I have. Like I mentioned a bit ago, there was a whole month where I wasn't having any of those mood problems at all. I was sleeping just fine, normal hours, and I wasn't wound up or making any of those big plans. My mood was just... quiet. But even during that whole month, I was still hearing my friend out on the porch, and I still knew the radio was sending messages to me. So, yes, the voices and the messages were still there even when my mood was completely normal.\n\nDoctor: About how long do your mood episodes usually last?\n\nPatient: Well, sir... those spells where I'm all wound up, hardly sleeping, and making all those big plans... they usually stick around for a while. If I had to guess, I'd say I've been dealing with those high-energy stretches coming and going for about 11 months now.\n\nDoctor: How old were you when the voices and messages first started?\n\nPatient: Well, sir... let me see. I'm 84 now, and... well, those voices and the special messages, they've been happening on and off for about 9 months now. So I suppose I was 83 when they first started up.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Give candidates as ICD-10 codes, not disease names.\n- If the list changed from the previous candidates, say in \"note\" what this turn added or ruled out. If it did not change, leave \"note\" empty.\n- If the candidates have narrowed to one and you are confident enough that no further questioning is needed, return true for is_final.\n\n[Output Format — JSON only, no other text]\n{\"candidates\": [\"<ICD-10 code>\", \"...\"], \"note\": \"<why the list changed, or empty>\", \"is_final\": <true or false>}\n"
  },

    "content": "Return the JSON now."
  }
